*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& S4 MATNR fix: material number input conversion via MATN1 (OMSL)
*& ALPHA = IN pads to MATNR field length (40) in S/4HANA - not 18
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
CLASS lcl_matnr DEFINITION FINAL.
  PUBLIC SECTION.
    CLASS-METHODS in IMPORTING iv_matnr        TYPE clike
                     RETURNING VALUE(rv_matnr) TYPE matnr.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS lcl_matnr IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD in.
    DATA(lv_in) = CONV matnr( iv_matnr ).
    CONDENSE lv_in.
    IF lv_in IS INITIAL.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_MATN1_INPUT'
      EXPORTING
        input        = lv_in
      IMPORTING
        output       = rv_matnr
      EXCEPTIONS
        length_error = 1
        OTHERS       = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      rv_matnr = lv_in.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

FORM f_sub_s911_line_item_data.
  "Code Remediations Begin of changes by ABAP7
  types: BEGIN OF ty_matnr,
         matnr type matnr18,
         vkorg type vkorg,
         END OF ty_matnr,
         tt_matnr type table of ty_matnr WITH DEFAULT KEY.
  "Code Remediations end of changes by ABAP7
  DATA: lv_date_temp   TYPE sy-datum,
        lv_startdate   TYPE sy-datum,
        lv_enddate     TYPE sy-datum,
        lv_startdate_1 TYPE sy-datum,
        lv_enddate_1   TYPE sy-datum,
        lv_exchrate    TYPE ukurs_curr.

  CLEAR: lv_date_temp,lv_startdate,lv_enddate,lv_startdate_1,lv_enddate_1.

  DATA(ta_s911_comb) = ta_s911[].
  IF NOT ta_s911_n1[] IS INITIAL.
    APPEND LINES OF ta_s911_n1 TO ta_s911_comb.
  ENDIF.
  "Fetch data from table T001
  DATA(lta_s911_vkorg) = ta_s911_comb.

  SORT lta_s911_vkorg BY vkorg.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lta_s911_vkorg COMPARING vkorg.

  IF NOT lta_s911_vkorg IS INITIAL.
    SELECT bukrs waers FROM t001
      INTO TABLE ta_t001
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lta_s911_vkorg
      WHERE bukrs = lta_s911_vkorg-vkorg.

    IF NOT ta_t001 IS INITIAL.
      SORT ta_t001 BY bukrs.

      IF s_spmon-low IS NOT INITIAL.
        lv_date_temp = |{ s_spmon-low }| & || & |{ co_01 } |.
        CONDENSE lv_date_temp.

        "Get first and last day of period
        CALL FUNCTION 'HR_JP_MONTH_BEGIN_END_DATE'
          EXPORTING
            iv_date             = lv_date_temp
          IMPORTING
            ev_month_begin_date = lv_startdate
            ev_month_end_date   = lv_enddate.

        "Convert Date
        CONVERT DATE lv_startdate INTO INVERTED-DATE lv_startdate_1.
        CONVERT DATE lv_enddate INTO INVERTED-DATE lv_enddate_1.
      ENDIF.

      "Fetch data from table TCURR
      SELECT * FROM tcurr
        INTO TABLE ta_tcurr2
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN ta_t001
        WHERE kurst = 'M'
          AND fcurr = ta_t001-waers
          AND tcurr = 'INR'
          AND gdatu GE lv_enddate_1 AND gdatu LE lv_startdate_1.

      "Calculate Exchange Rate
      IF NOT ta_tcurr2 IS INITIAL.

        SORT ta_tcurr2 BY fcurr.
        DATA(ta_curr1_temp) = ta_tcurr2.
        DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM ta_curr1_temp COMPARING fcurr.

        LOOP AT ta_curr1_temp INTO DATA(wa_tcurr1_temp).

          DATA(ta_exch) = ta_tcurr2.
          DELETE ta_exch WHERE fcurr <> wa_tcurr1_temp-fcurr.

          IF NOT ta_exch IS INITIAL.

            DESCRIBE TABLE ta_exch LINES DATA(lv_exch_lines).

            lv_exchrate = REDUCE ukurs_curr( INIT x = '0.0000000'  FOR wa_exch IN ta_exch NEXT x = x + wa_exch-ukurs ).
            IF lv_exch_lines IS NOT INITIAL.
              lv_exchrate = lv_exchrate / lv_exch_lines.
            ENDIF.

            wa_exch_tab-waers = wa_tcurr1_temp-fcurr.
            wa_exch_tab-ukurs = lv_exchrate.

            APPEND wa_exch_tab TO ta_exch_tab.
          ENDIF.

          CLEAR: wa_exch_tab,lv_exchrate,lv_exch_lines,wa_tcurr1_temp.
          REFRESH: ta_exch[].
        ENDLOOP.

        IF NOT ta_exch_tab IS INITIAL.
          SORT ta_exch_tab BY waers.
        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  "Fetch data from table ZCO_MIDAS_STCON
  IF ta_s911_comb IS NOT INITIAL. " Change added by Inc Dummy | TR CRDK985892 | Prathamesh Parab | 06.01.2023
    SELECT * FROM zco_midas_stcon
      INTO TABLE ta_stcon
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN ta_s911_comb
      WHERE plaag_01 = ta_s911_comb-plaag_01.
    IF NOT ta_stcon IS INITIAL.
      SORT ta_stcon BY plaag_01.
    ENDIF.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SUB_TVKBT_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_sub_tvkbt_data .
  IF ta_s911 IS NOT INITIAL.
    DATA : lta_s911 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_s911 INITIAL SIZE 0.
    lta_s911 = ta_s911.
    SORT lta_s911 BY vkbur.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lta_s911 COMPARING vkbur.
    SELECT vkbur
           bezei
           FROM tvkbt
           INTO TABLE ta_tvkbt
           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lta_s911
           WHERE vkbur = lta_s911-vkbur
           AND   spras = co_en.
  ENDIF.

  IF ta_knvv IS NOT INITIAL.
    DATA(lt_knvv) = ta_knvv.
    SORT lt_knvv BY vkbur.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_knvv COMPARING vkbur.
    SELECT vkbur
            bezei
            FROM tvkbt
            APPENDING TABLE ta_tvkbt
            FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lt_knvv
            WHERE vkbur = lt_knvv-vkbur
            AND   spras = co_en.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.

          wa_final-edns_lc           = wa_final-edns_m * wa_final-lc_rate.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SUB_FCAT_CREATE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_1447   text
*      -->P_1448   text
*      -->P_1449   text
*      -->P_1450   text
*      -->P_1451   text
*      -->P_1452   text
*      -->P_1453   text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_sub_fcat_create  USING    u_col  TYPE sy-cucol
                               u_fnam TYPE slis_fieldname
                               u_seltxt TYPE dd03p-scrtext_m
                               u_outlen TYPE dd03p-outputlen
                               u_tab  TYPE slis_tabname
                               u_dosum TYPE c.
  w_fcat-col_pos =  u_col.
  w_fcat-fieldname = u_fnam.
  w_fcat-tabname = u_tab.
  w_fcat-seltext_m = u_seltxt.
  w_fcat-do_sum = u_dosum.
  w_fcat-outputlen = u_outlen.
  APPEND w_fcat TO t_fcat.
  CLEAR w_fcat.
ENDFORM.

FORM f_top_of_page.
  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_COMMENTARY_WRITE'
    EXPORTING
      it_list_commentary = t_header
*     I_LOGO             =
*     I_END_OF_LIST_GRID =
*     I_ALV_FORM         =
    .

ENDFORM.

FORM f_set_pf_status USING u_rt_extab TYPE slis_t_extab.

  SET PF-STATUS 'ZSTANDARD_FULLSCREEN'.

ENDFORM. "set_pf_status

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SUB_FETCH_VBRK_VBRP_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_sub_fetch_vbrk_vbrp_data .
  DATA : lv_i_date  TYPE dats.

  DATA : lv_i_month TYPE  fcmnr,
         lv_i_year  TYPE  gjahr.


  SELECT fkart
         FROM tvfk
         INTO TABLE @DATA(t_tvfk)
         WHERE fkart IN @s_fkart.
  IF t_tvfk IS NOT INITIAL.
    LOOP AT s_fkart.
      READ TABLE t_tvfk INTO DATA(w_tvfk) WITH KEY fkart = s_fkart-low.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        r_blart-low = s_fkart-low.
        r_blart-sign = 'I'(279).
        r_blart-option = 'EQ'.
        APPEND r_blart.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.




  IF s_spmon-low IS NOT INITIAL AND s_spmon-high IS NOT INITIAL.
    lv_i_month  = s_spmon-low+4(2).
    lv_i_year = s_spmon-low+0(4).
    CONCATENATE s_spmon-low co_01 INTO lv_i_date.
    CALL FUNCTION 'OIL_MONTH_GET_FIRST_LAST'
      EXPORTING
        i_month     = lv_i_month
        i_year      = lv_i_year
        i_date      = lv_i_date
      IMPORTING
        e_first_day = r_date-low
*       E_LAST_DAY  = s_date-high
      EXCEPTIONS
        wrong_date  = 1
        OTHERS      = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
    ENDIF.
*s_date-sign = 'I'.
*s_date-option = 'EQ'.
*append s_date.


    CLEAR : lv_i_month, lv_i_year, lv_i_date.
    lv_i_month  = s_spmon-high+4(2).
    lv_i_year = s_spmon-high+0(4).
    CONCATENATE s_spmon-high co_01 INTO lv_i_date.
    CALL FUNCTION 'OIL_MONTH_GET_FIRST_LAST'
      EXPORTING
        i_month    = lv_i_month
        i_year     = lv_i_year
        i_date     = lv_i_date
      IMPORTING
*       E_FIRST_DAY       = s_date-low
        e_last_day = r_date-high
      EXCEPTIONS
        wrong_date = 1
        OTHERS     = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
    ENDIF.
    r_date-sign = 'I'(279).
    r_date-option = 'BT'.
    APPEND r_date.
  ELSEIF s_spmon-low IS NOT INITIAL AND s_spmon-high IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR : lv_i_month, lv_i_year, lv_i_date.
    lv_i_month  = s_spmon-low+4(2).
    lv_i_year = s_spmon-low+0(4).
    CONCATENATE s_spmon-low co_01 INTO lv_i_date.
    CALL FUNCTION 'OIL_MONTH_GET_FIRST_LAST'
      EXPORTING
        i_month     = lv_i_month
        i_year      = lv_i_year
        i_date      = lv_i_date
      IMPORTING
        e_first_day = r_date-low
        e_last_day  = r_date-high
      EXCEPTIONS
        wrong_date  = 1
        OTHERS      = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
    ENDIF.
    r_date-sign = 'I'(279).
    r_date-option = 'BT'.
    APPEND r_date.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.
"Added on 10.11.2017 by deepak ojha CR#8000003906


*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SUB_FETCH_PRC_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_sub_fetch_prc_data .
  IF ta_s911 IS NOT INITIAL.
    DATA : lta_s911      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_s911 INITIAL SIZE 0,
           lta_s911_land TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_s911,
           lr_land_rng   TYPE RANGE OF land1,
           wa_land_rng   LIKE LINE OF lr_land_rng.

    lta_s911 = ta_s911.
    lta_s911_land = ta_s911.

    SORT lta_s911_land BY plaag_01.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lta_s911_land COMPARING plaag_01.

    LOOP AT lta_s911_land INTO DATA(lwa_s911_tmp).
      wa_land_rng-sign = 'I'.
      wa_land_rng-option = 'EQ'.
      wa_land_rng-low = lwa_s911_tmp-plaag_01.
      APPEND wa_land_rng TO lr_land_rng.
      CLEAR wa_land_rng.
    ENDLOOP.

    SORT lta_s911 BY matnr pkunag." pkunag plaag_01.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lta_s911 COMPARING matnr pkunag." pkunag plaag_01.
    "SOA by Debasmita - 8000007907 - 14.04.2023
    LOOP AT lta_s911 ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<lfs_s911>).
      <lfs_s911>-pkunag = |{ <lfs_s911>-pkunag ALPHA = IN } |.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF lta_s911 IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT reg_cd,
             item,
             v_no,
             fgcode,
             b_type,
             sales_cust,
             marketstat
             FROM zsd_gpl_bd
             INTO TABLE @DATA(ta_bd_data)
             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lta_s911
             WHERE fgcode     = @lta_s911-matnr
             AND   sales_cust = @lta_s911-pkunag.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        SORT ta_bd_data BY reg_cd item v_no.
        SELECT reg_cd,
               item,
               v_no,
               land,
               country,
               ma_holder,
               ma_no,
               reg_status,
               pack_size,
               dosage,
               zdate,
               mol_code,
               mol_nm,
               therapy_name
          FROM zsd_gpl_ra
          INTO TABLE @DATA(ta_ra_data)
          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @ta_bd_data
          WHERE reg_cd = @ta_bd_data-reg_cd AND
                item   = @ta_bd_data-item AND
                v_no = @ta_bd_data-v_no AND
                land IN @lr_land_rng.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          SORT ta_ra_data BY reg_cd item v_no.

          SELECT reg_cd,
                 item,
                 v_no,
                 sdea
            FROM zsd_gpl_le
            INTO TABLE @DATA(ta_le_data)
            FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @ta_ra_data
            WHERE reg_cd = @ta_ra_data-reg_cd AND
                  item   = @ta_ra_data-item AND
                  v_no = @ta_ra_data-v_no.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            SORT ta_le_data BY reg_cd item v_no.
          ENDIF.

          SELECT reg_cd,
                 item,
                 v_no,
                 sdea_legal
          FROM zsd_gpl_qa
            INTO TABLE @DATA(ta_qa_data)
            FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @ta_ra_data
          WHERE reg_cd = @ta_ra_data-reg_cd AND
                item   = @ta_ra_data-item AND
                v_no = @ta_ra_data-v_no.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            SORT ta_qa_data BY reg_cd item v_no.
          ENDIF.

          LOOP AT ta_ra_data INTO DATA(wa_ra_data).
            wa_prc_data_tmp-v_no         = wa_ra_data-v_no.
            wa_prc_data_tmp-land         = wa_ra_data-land.
            wa_prc_data_tmp-country      = wa_ra_data-country.
            wa_prc_data_tmp-ma_holder    = wa_ra_data-ma_holder.
            wa_prc_data_tmp-ma_no        = wa_ra_data-ma_no.
            wa_prc_data_tmp-reg_status   = wa_ra_data-reg_status.
            wa_prc_data_tmp-pack_size    = wa_ra_data-pack_size.
            wa_prc_data_tmp-dosage       = wa_ra_data-dosage.
            wa_prc_data_tmp-zdate       = wa_ra_data-zdate.
            wa_prc_data_tmp-mol_code     = wa_ra_data-mol_code.
            wa_prc_data_tmp-mol_nm       = wa_ra_data-mol_nm.
            wa_prc_data_tmp-therapy_name = wa_ra_data-therapy_name.


            READ TABLE ta_bd_data INTO DATA(wa_bd_data) WITH KEY reg_cd = wa_ra_data-reg_cd
                                                                 item = wa_ra_data-item
                                                                 v_no = wa_ra_data-v_no
                                                                 BINARY SEARCH.
            IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
              wa_prc_data_tmp-fgcode     = wa_bd_data-fgcode.
              wa_prc_data_tmp-sales_cust = |{ wa_bd_data-sales_cust ALPHA = OUT } |.
              wa_prc_data_tmp-b_type     = wa_bd_data-b_type.
              wa_prc_data_tmp-marketstat = wa_bd_data-marketstat.

            ENDIF.

            READ TABLE ta_le_data INTO DATA(wa_le_data) WITH KEY reg_cd = wa_ra_data-reg_cd
                                                                 item = wa_ra_data-item
                                                                 v_no = wa_ra_data-v_no
                                                                 BINARY SEARCH.
            IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
              wa_prc_data_tmp-sdea = wa_le_data-sdea.
            ENDIF.

            READ TABLE ta_qa_data INTO DATA(wa_qa_data) WITH KEY reg_cd = wa_ra_data-reg_cd
                                                                 item = wa_ra_data-item
                                                                 v_no = wa_ra_data-v_no
                                                                 BINARY SEARCH.
            IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
              wa_prc_data_tmp-sdea_legal = wa_qa_data-sdea_legal.
            ENDIF.
            APPEND wa_prc_data_tmp TO ta_prc_data.
            CLEAR wa_prc_data_tmp.
          ENDLOOP.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
*    SELECT fgcode
*           sales_cust
*           land
*           country
*           ma_no
*           ma_holder
*           pack_size
*           dosage
*           mol_code
*           mol_nm
*           therapy_name
*           reg_status
*           marketstat
*           sdea
*           sdea_legal
*           b_type
*           zdate
*           v_no
*           FROM zprc_data
*           INTO TABLE ta_prc_data
*           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lta_s911
*           WHERE fgcode     = lta_s911-matnr.
*           AND   sales_cust = lt_s911-pkunag
*           AND   land       = lt_s911-plaag_01.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
*      SORT ta_prc_data BY zdate fgcode DESCENDING.
*      SORT ta_prc_data BY fgcode sales_cust land v_no DESCENDING. "CRDK961424
        SORT ta_prc_data BY fgcode sales_cust land DESCENDING.
      ENDIF.
*      End of change by Debasmita - 8000007907 - 14.04.2023
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.



*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

FORM f_sub_create_heading USING u_descrption.
  wa_ddfields_1-fieldtext = u_descrption.
  APPEND wa_ddfields_1 TO  ta_ddfields_1  .
  CLEAR wa_ddfields_1.

ENDFORM.


*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SUB_FETCH_SEGMENT_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_sub_fetch_segment_data .
  DATA(lt_s911) = ta_s911.
  APPEND LINES OF ta_s911_n1 TO lt_s911.
  SORT lt_s911 BY ip_vend sakn1.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_s911 COMPARING ip_vend spmon sakn1.
  IF lt_s911 IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT bukrs
           belnr
           gjahr
           buzei
           wrbtr
           dmbtr
           hkont
           shkzg
           kunnr
           spmon
           kvgr1
           segment
           FROM zsd_doc_segment
           INTO TABLE ta_segment
           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lt_s911
           WHERE belnr = lt_s911-ip_vend
*           AND   gjahr = lt_s911-spmon+0(4)
           AND   hkont = lt_s911-sakn1
           AND   kvgr1 <> ' '.
  ENDIF.

  IF ta_segment IS  NOT INITIAL.
    DATA(lt_segment) = ta_segment.
    SORT lt_segment BY kvgr1.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_segment COMPARING kvgr1.
    SELECT kvgr1
           bezei
           FROM tvv1t
           INTO TABLE ta_tvv1t_n
           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lt_segment
           WHERE kvgr1 = lt_segment-kvgr1
            AND  spras = co_en.

    SELECT kvgr1
           bezei
           FROM tvv1t
           APPENDING TABLE ta_tvv1t_n
           WHERE kvgr1 IN ( p_kvgr1, co_07 )
            AND  spras = co_en.
  ENDIF.
  SORT ta_tvv1t_n BY kvgr1.
ENDFORM.

          wa_final-edns_lc           = wa_final-edns_m * wa_final-lc_rate.
          wa_final-total_cost_lc     = wa_final-total_cost * wa_final-lc_rate.

    IF rd_4 = 'X' AND ta_ftp IS NOT INITIAL.
      DATA: lv_applpath TYPE char200.
      SELECT SINGLE param_name FROM zparam_table INTO @DATA(l_path_r3) WHERE id_name = @co_midas AND
                                                                            id_number = '3'.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        MESSAGE 'Please maintain path for AL11 folder'(477) TYPE 'E'.
      ENDIF.
      CONCATENATE l_path_r3 'S911_REPORT_' s_spmon-low+4(2) s_spmon-low+0(4) '_' s_spmon-high+4(2) s_spmon-high+0(4) '_' sy-datum sy-uzeit '.TXT'(284) INTO lv_applpath.
      OPEN DATASET lv_applpath FOR OUTPUT IN TEXT MODE ENCODING DEFAULT.
      LOOP AT ta_ftp INTO DATA(wa_ftp).
        REPLACE '#' IN wa_ftp WITH '|'.

      ENDLOOP.
      CLOSE DATASET lv_applpath.

    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.


*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FTP_CONNECT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_G_DEST  text
*      <--P_G_HDL  text
*      <--P_EXP5  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM ftp_connect  USING u_l_dest
                 CHANGING c_l_hdl
                          c_l_exp.
*&--Added on 30.10.2018 CR#8000004914
  IF rd_6 EQ abap_true.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY ABAP7 27.06.2026 FOR ATC
*    SELECT SINGLE userid
*                    username
*                    password
*                    host
*        FROM zsd_ftp INTO wa_sd_ftp WHERE userid = co_sap.
    SELECT userid username password host
      UP TO 1 ROWS INTO wa_sd_ftp
      FROM zsd_ftp
      WHERE userid = co_sap
      ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
    ENDSELECT.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_A Conversion **END OF CHANGE BY ABAP7 27.06.2026 FOR ATC
  ELSE.
*&--End added on 30.10.2018 CR#8000004914
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY ABAP7 27.06.2026 FOR ATC
*    SELECT SINGLE userid
*                  username
*                  password
*                  host
*      FROM zsd_ftp INTO wa_sd_ftp WHERE userid = co_midas.
    SELECT userid username password host
      UP TO 1 ROWS INTO wa_sd_ftp
      FROM zsd_ftp
      WHERE userid = co_midas
      ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
    ENDSELECT.
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_A Conversion **END OF CHANGE BY ABAP7 27.06.2026 FOR ATC
  ENDIF.
  DATA(lv_user) = wa_sd_ftp-username.
  DATA(lv_pwd) = wa_sd_ftp-password.
  DATA(lv_host) = wa_sd_ftp-host.

  DATA : lv_key  TYPE i VALUE 26101957,
         lv_slen TYPE i.

  lv_slen = strlen( lv_pwd ).
  CALL FUNCTION 'HTTP_SCRAMBLE'
    EXPORTING
      source      = lv_pwd
      sourcelen   = lv_slen
      key         = lv_key
    IMPORTING
      destination = lv_pwd.

  CALL FUNCTION 'FTP_CONNECT'
    EXPORTING
      user            = lv_user
      password        = lv_pwd
      host            = lv_host
      rfc_destination = u_l_dest
    IMPORTING
      handle          = c_l_hdl
    EXCEPTIONS
      OTHERS          = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    c_l_exp = 'X'.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SEND_TO_FTP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LV_FILE  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_send_to_ftp  USING    u_lv_file.

  CALL FUNCTION 'FTP_R3_TO_SERVER'
    EXPORTING
      handle         = gv_g_hdl
      fname          = u_lv_file          "file path of destination system
      character_mode = 'X'
    TABLES
      text           = ta_ftp
    EXCEPTIONS
      tcpip_error    = 1
      command_error  = 2
      data_error     = 3
      OTHERS         = 4.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
    WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4
    RAISING invalid_output_file.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FTP_DISCONNECT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_G_HDL  text
*      -->P_G_DEST  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM ftp_disconnect  USING    u_l_hdl
                              u_l_dest.

  CALL FUNCTION 'FTP_DISCONNECT'
    EXPORTING
      handle = u_l_hdl.

  CALL FUNCTION 'RFC_CONNECTION_CLOSE'
    EXPORTING
      destination = u_l_dest
    EXCEPTIONS
      OTHERS      = 1.

ENDFORM.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_SUB_FETCH_COST_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_sub_fetch_cost_data .
  DATA(lt_s911) = ta_s911.
  DELETE lt_s911 WHERE matnr IS INITIAL.
  DELETE lt_s911 WHERE ip_vend NS co_plnt.
  SORT lt_s911 BY matnr ip_vend.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_s911 COMPARING matnr ip_vend.
  IF lt_s911 IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT matnr
           werks
           klvar
           api_mat
           api_mat_p
           e_mat
           p_mat
           api_oh_v
           for_oh
           api_oh_f
           f_oh_f
           FROM zco_cost_breakup
           INTO TABLE ta_cost
           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lt_s911
           WHERE matnr = lt_s911-matnr
            AND  werks = lt_s911-ip_vend+4(4)
            AND  klvar = co_ppc1.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT ta_cost BY matnr werks.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_SUB_DELETE_SELECTION
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_sub_delete_selection .
  IF ta_s911 IS NOT INITIAL AND s_vkorg[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE ta_s911 WHERE vkorg NOT IN s_vkorg.
  ENDIF.

  IF ta_s911 IS NOT INITIAL AND s_vtweg[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE ta_s911 WHERE vtweg NOT IN s_vtweg.
  ENDIF.

  IF ta_s911 IS NOT INITIAL AND s_spart[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE ta_s911 WHERE spart NOT IN s_spart.
  ENDIF.

  IF ta_s911 IS NOT INITIAL AND s_sakn1[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE ta_s911 WHERE sakn1 NOT IN s_sakn1.
  ENDIF.

  IF ta_s911 IS NOT INITIAL AND s_kunag[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE ta_s911 WHERE  pkunag NOT IN  s_kunag.
  ENDIF.

  IF ta_s911 IS NOT INITIAL AND s_vkbur[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE ta_s911 WHERE vkbur NOT IN s_vkbur.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.

*       text

*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_sub_fetch_mara_data .

  DATA(ta_s911_m) = ta_s911.
  APPEND LINES OF ta_s911_n1 TO ta_s911_m.
  SORT ta_s911_m BY matnr.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM ta_s911_m COMPARING matnr.


  SELECT matnr
         meins
    INTO TABLE ta_mara
    FROM mara
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN ta_s911_m
    WHERE matnr = ta_s911_m-matnr.

ENDFORM.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_BU
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_bu.
  CLEAR gv_msg.
  DATA(lta_s911) = ta_s911.
  SORT lta_s911 BY kvgr1.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lta_s911 COMPARING kvgr1.

  SELECT sapid,
         bu
    FROM zcomidas_bu_auth
    INTO TABLE @DATA(ta_zcomidas_bu_auth)
    WHERE sapid = @sy-uname.

  IF NOT ta_zcomidas_bu_auth IS INITIAL.
    SORT ta_zcomidas_bu_auth BY sapid bu.
    READ TABLE ta_zcomidas_bu_auth INTO DATA(wa_zcomidas_bu_auth) WITH KEY sapid = sy-uname
                                                                           bu = co_*
                                                                           BINARY SEARCH
                                                                           TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      "DO Nothing

    ELSE.
      LOOP AT lta_s911 INTO DATA(lwa_s911).
        READ TABLE ta_zcomidas_bu_auth INTO DATA(wa_zcomidas_bu_auth1) WITH KEY sapid = sy-uname
                                                                                bu = lwa_s911-kvgr1
                                                                                BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CONCATENATE co_bu lwa_s911-kvgr1 INTO gv_msg SEPARATED BY space.
          MESSAGE gv_msg TYPE co_s DISPLAY LIKE co_e.
          LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR: wa_zcomidas_bu_auth1,lwa_s911.
      ENDLOOP.

    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    MESSAGE text-476 TYPE co_s DISPLAY LIKE co_e.
    LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

"Code Remediations Begin of changes by ABAP7 29.10.2026 dump TSV_TNEW_PAGE_ALLOC_FAILED
*  DATA(ta_final_line) = ta_final.

  TYPES: BEGIN OF lty_rec_no,
           spmon  TYPE zsd_s911_line_it-spmon,
           record TYPE zsd_s911_line_it-record,
         END OF lty_rec_no.
  CONSTANTS lc_pack_size TYPE i VALUE 20000.
  DATA: lt_rec_no  TYPE HASHED TABLE OF lty_rec_no WITH UNIQUE KEY spmon,
        lt_rec_db  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF lty_rec_no,
        lr_spmon   TYPE RANGE OF zsd_s911_line_it-spmon,
        lv_upd_err TYPE abap_bool,
        lv_upd_cnt TYPE i.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <ls_rec_no> TYPE lty_rec_no.

* Periods of this run (few entries) - no copy and no SORT of TA_FINAL
  LOOP AT ta_final ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_final_p>).
    INSERT VALUE #( spmon = <ls_final_p>-spmon ) INTO TABLE lt_rec_no.
  ENDLOOP.
  CHECK lt_rec_no IS NOT INITIAL.
  lr_spmon = VALUE #( FOR ls_p IN lt_rec_no ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = ls_p-spmon ) ).

* Last record number per period - one aggregate statement (HANA push-down)
  SELECT spmon, MAX( record ) AS record
    FROM zsd_s911_line_it
    WHERE spmon IN @lr_spmon
    GROUP BY spmon
    INTO TABLE @lt_rec_db.
  LOOP AT lt_rec_db ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_rec_db>).
    ASSIGN lt_rec_no[ spmon = <ls_rec_db>-spmon ] TO <ls_rec_no>.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      <ls_rec_no>-record = <ls_rec_db>-record.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  FREE: lt_rec_db, lr_spmon.

  LOOP AT ta_final INTO wa_final.

*   Next record number of the period (1 when the period is new)
    ASSIGN lt_rec_no[ spmon = wa_final-spmon ] TO <ls_rec_no>.
    <ls_rec_no>-record = <ls_rec_no>-record + 1.
    lv_record = <ls_rec_no>-record.

"SOA by Pooja - CR#8000007907 - 05.05.2022
FORM fill_spmon1  USING lv_spmon TYPE  zfi_exp_prov-spmon1
      CHANGING lv_spmon1 TYPE zfi_exp_prov-spmon1.

  DATA : lv_months_back TYPE numc3 VALUE '001',
         lv_date_back   TYPE sy-datum.
  lv_date_temp = |{ lv_spmon }| & || & |{ '01' } |.
  CONDENSE lv_date_temp.

  CALL FUNCTION 'CCM_GO_BACK_MONTHS'
    EXPORTING
      currdate   = lv_date_temp
      backmonths = lv_months_back
    IMPORTING
      newdate    = lv_date_back.


  CALL FUNCTION 'DATE_TO_PERIOD_CONVERT'
    EXPORTING
      i_date         = lv_date_temp
      i_periv        = 'V3'
    IMPORTING
      e_buper        = lv_period
      e_gjahr        = lv_fy
    EXCEPTIONS
      input_false    = 1
      t009_notfound  = 2
      t009b_notfound = 3
      OTHERS         = 4.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    CLEAR lv_spmon1.
    lv_fimonth = lv_period.
    CONCATENATE lv_fy lv_fimonth INTO lv_spmon1.
    CLEAR:lv_date_back,lv_date_temp,lv_fimonth,lv_fy,lv_period.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.

FORM fi_spmon  USING lv_spmon TYPE s911-spmon
      CHANGING lv_spmonfi TYPE zfi_exp_prov-spmon1.

  lv_date = |{ lv_spmon }| & || & |{ '01' } |.
  CALL FUNCTION 'DATE_TO_PERIOD_CONVERT'
    EXPORTING
      i_date         = lv_date
      i_periv        = 'V3'
    IMPORTING
      e_buper        = lv_period
      e_gjahr        = lv_fy
    EXCEPTIONS
      input_false    = 1
      t009_notfound  = 2
      t009b_notfound = 3
      OTHERS         = 4.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
  ENDIF.

  lv_fimonth = lv_period.
  CONCATENATE lv_fy lv_fimonth INTO lv_spmonfi.
ENDFORM.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_SUB_IP_CODE_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_sub_ip_code_data.

  SELECT sku_code
         zone_sac
         comp_code
         ip_code
  FROM zco_sac_mat_mast
  INTO TABLE ta_zco_sac_mat_mast
  FOR ALL ENTRIES IN ta_s911
  WHERE  sku_code = ta_s911-matnr
  AND comp_code = ta_s911-vkorg.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    SORT ta_zco_sac_mat_mast BY sku_code zone_sac comp_code.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
